# AFRIK corpus consistency report, November 2026: full lists

The complete lists behind the longest sections of [the report](corpus-consistency-2026-11.md). Same measurement, same order, same conventions; read the report first for the method and for what each section means.

## One address, several titles

634 findings, shown on 791 pages with 65 measured visitors.

Each row is one address cited under more than one title. For chapters of the UNESCO _Histoire générale de l'Afrique_, which share one address per volume, a row is one chapter. Titles are listed by how often they are used; the order is not a choice between them. For the most used title, the count is given; for every other title, each place it is used.

## Surname fiche links a people that never writes the surname

612 findings, shown on 693 pages with 29 measured visitors.

The surname fiche links the surname to a people (`peoples[].peopleId`); the people fiche never writes the surname, in any of its spellings. People fiches have no surname field, so the back link can only be prose. Grouped by people fiche.
